[Test Tunnels]
A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.
You see a giant termite (dead). A dagger, a chest, a chest, a dagger, a dagger,
a huge damaged carnelian, a tiny citrine and a copper coin are here.
Obvious exits: north, west, southeast.

walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.

[Test Tunnels]
A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.
A dagger, a chest, a chest, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger, a huge damaged
carnelian, a tiny citrine, a jade flask, some silver coins and some copper
coins are here.
Obvious exits: north, west, southeast.

[Test Tunnels]
A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.
You see a kobold. A dagger, a chest, a chest, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger, a
huge damaged carnelian, a tiny citrine, a jade flask, a dagger, a dagger, a
dagger, a strongbox, a bloodstone, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger, a
tiny carnelian, a dagger, a dagger, a bloodstone, a silver coin, a dagger and a
copper coin are here.

A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.

[Test Tunnels]
A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.
You see a metal devil and a metal devil. A dagger, a chest, a chest, a dagger,
a dagger, a dagger, a huge damaged carnelian, a tiny citrine, a jade flask, a

agate, a dagger, a chipped carnelian, a chipped obsidian, a jade flask, a
dagger, a dagger, a bloodstone, a dagger, a dagger, a trunk, a dagger, a
dagger, a dagger, an amethyst, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger, a small
bloodstone, a large obsidian, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger, a sack, a flawless
carnelian, a sack, a dagger, a sack, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger, a chest, a

A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.

walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.
You see a metal devil and a death kobold. A tiny citrine, a jade flask, an
obsidian flask, a jade flask and a crystal bottle are here.
Obvious exits: north, west, southeast.

[Test Tunnels]
A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.
You see a metal devil (dead) and a giant termite (dead). A dagger, a dagger, a
tiny citrine, a jade flask, an obsidian flask, a jade flask, a crystal bottle,
a bag, a dagger, a tiny chipped jasper, a dagger, a dagger, a chipped citrine
and a dagger are here.
Obvious exits: north, west, southeast.

> look
[Test Tunnels]
A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.
A dagger, a dagger, a tiny citrine, a jade flask, an obsidian flask, a jade
flask, a marble vial, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger, a chest, a crystal bottle,
a chest, a bag, a dagger, a tiny chipped jasper, a dagger, a dagger, a dagger,
a small chipped moonstone, a chipped citrine and a dagger are here.
Obvious exits: north, west, southeast.

[Test Tunnels]
A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.

A long, stone hallway stretches out in many directions from this point. The
walls and floor of this hallway have been painted in in a grayish green color.
The paint has started to chip and flake in numerous spots along the walls.
Fixed to the ceiling, and running the length of the hallway are numerous metal
pipes, their purpose unknown. On some of the walls various
forms of graffiti can be made out. Many identical posters depicting a kobold
with an enlarged forhead cover the walls. There is a deep fissure in the floor
here.

[Home Sweet Home, living area]
As you step into this warm cabin, a wonderful scent greets you, faintly
tugging at your memory. Shirred white curtains are pulled back from the
window, letting light in to glow softly against the light wood of the
walls. Many shades of greens mix for a pleasant effect in the rug and throw
pillows. At the opposite end of the room is the table and eating area. A
beautiful painting over the plush sofa draws your eye. Everything seems to
be in its place, but still comfortable and inviting. It is evident this is
someone's well loved Home. A stone fireplace and a cradle are here.
An amethyst, a chipped citrine, a bone wand, a bloodstone, a bloodstone, a
large amethyst, a faceted jasper, a chipped obsidian, a bloodstone, an

tiny chipped citrine, an agate, a coral barrette, a chipped bloodstone, a tiny

[Key'n Anchor, Locksmith's Workshop]
This corner of the room is a bit less cluttered than the remainder of the shop.
The three walls are each lined with a short work bench upon which are a large
assortment of fine tools and a vast array of lock mechanisms in various states
of repair or disarray. A few strongboxes, chests and coffers are atop the far
bench, their lids open and locks removed. Many more are stacked beneath the
benches, awaiting repair or purchase. A very short stool sits in front of a
small, well-worn machine. An iron spiral stairway winds down beneath the shop.
A small manuscript, bound in leather, hangs tacked to a nearby wall.
